Poki takes all necessary precautions and security measures to help ensure your safety and that of other players. Furthermore, every game on our platform has gone through a thorough quality assurance process. However, we can only guarantee your safety on our website. If you decide to leave our website through external links, we cannot guarantee your safety. Poki runs completely in your browser, which is a safe fenced-off environment within your computer. Without permission, it is not possible to access files or install software on your device. For more information, we advise you to read our \u003Ca href=\\\\\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fc\u002Fterms-of-use\\\\\\\" target=\\\\\\\"_blank\\\\\\\" rel=\\\\\\\"noopener\\\\\\\"\u003ETerms of Use\u003C\u002Fa\u003E.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\\"\\n    },\\n    {\\n      \\\"question\\\": \\\"What can I do if I see an inappropriate ad?\\\",\\n      \\\"answer\\\": \\\"\u003Cp\u003EThe advertisements on our website are restricted to appropriate content only. If you nevertheless come across any inappropriate content, please let us know by sending an email to: \u003Ca href=\\\\\\\"mailto:hello@poki.com\\\\\\\" target=\\\\\\\"_blank\\\\\\\" rel=\\\\\\\"noopener\\\\\\\"\u003Ehello@poki.com\u003C\u002Fa\u003E. Any screenshots, links to the concerning advertisement, and other relevant information is also appreciated. This will help us speed up the process of finding and restricting these advertisements.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\\"\\n    },\\n    {\\n      \\\"question\\\": \\\"Can you get viruses on Poki?\\\",\\n      \\\"answer\\\": \\\"\u003Cp\u003ENo, Poki runs completely in your browser and we never ask you to download anything. It is therefore virtually impossible to get viruses from playing Poki in-browser games. However, there are some websites out there that pretend to be Poki (they steal our design, try to look like our homepage, use the same colors, etc.). We cannot guarantee your safety on other websites. Poki takes all necessary precautions and security measures to help ensure your safety and that of other players. Furthermore, every game on our platform has gone through a thorough quality assurance process. However, we can only guarantee your safety on our website. If you decide to leave our website through external links, we cannot guarantee your safety. Poki runs completely in your browser, which is a safe fenced-off environment within your computer. Without permission, it is not possible to access files or install software on your device. For more information, we advise you to read our \u003Ca href=\\\\\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fc\u002Fterms-of-use\\\\\\\" target=\\\\\\\"_blank\\\\\\\" rel=\\\\\\\"noopener\\\\\\\"\u003ETerms of Use\u003C\u002Fa\u003E.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\\"\\n    },\\n    {\\n      \\\"question\\\": \\\"What can I do if I see an inappropriate ad?\\\",\\n      \\\"answer\\\": \\\"\u003Cp\u003EThe advertisements on our website are restricted to appropriate content only. That means we do our best to prevent and remove any content that is defamatory, indecent, obscene, harassing, violent, or otherwise objectionable material. If you nevertheless come across any inappropriate content, please let us know by sending an email to: \u003Ca href=\\\\\\\"mailto:hello@poki.com\\\\\\\" target=\\\\\\\"_blank\\\\\\\" rel=\\\\\\\"noopener\\\\\\\"\u003Ehello@poki.com\u003C\u002Fa\u003E. Any screenshots, links to the concerning advertisement, and other relevant information is also appreciated. This will help us speed up the process of finding and restricting these advertisements.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\\"\\n    },\\n    {\\n      \\\"question\\\": \\\"Can you get viruses on Poki?\\\",\\n      \\\"answer\\\": \\\"\u003Cp\u003ENo, Poki runs completely in your browser and we never ask you to download anything. It is therefore virtually impossible to get viruses from playing Poki in-browser games. However, there are some websites out there that pretend to be Poki (they steal our design, try to look like our homepage, use the same colors, etc.). We cannot guarantee your safety on other websites. If you come across such websites, please do not hesitate to contact us at: \u003Ca href=\\\\\\\"mailto:hello@poki.com\\\\\\\" target=\\\\\\\"_blank\\\\\\\" rel=\\\\\\\"noopener\\\\\\\"\u003Ehello@poki.com\u003C\u002Fa\u003E.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\\"\\n    },\\n    {\\n      \\\"question\\\": \\\"Do I have to download or install something to play?\\\",\\n      \\\"answer\\\": \\\"\u003Cp\u003ENever! All of the games are available to play on mobile, tablet and desktop.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003ETry driving games like Drift Boss, where one wrong turn sends you off the edge, or skill games like Stickman Hook, where perfect timing keeps your swing alive. There are also multiplayer games like Smash Karts, where you race and battle other players in real time.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EEach month, over \u003Cstrong\u003E100 million players join Poki to play\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E, share and find fun games to play on the web.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EWanna play? Start with what’s trending right now!\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Ch2\u003ETop free games\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EThese are the \u003Cstrong\u003E5 top trending games\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E on Poki according to live stats on what’s being played the most right now.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Col\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fg\u002Flevel-devil\\\"\u003ELevel Devil\u003C\u002Fa\u003E - 4.4⭐\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fg\u002Fsmash-karts\\\"\u003ESmash Karts\u003C\u002Fa\u003E - 4.4⭐\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fg\u002Fsubway-surfers\\\"\u003ESubway Surfers\u003C\u002Fa\u003E - 4.4⭐\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fg\u002Fblocky-blast-puzzle\\\"\u003EBlocky Blast Puzzle\u003C\u002Fa\u003E - 4.3⭐\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fg\u002Fdrive-mad\\\"\u003EDrive Mad\u003C\u002Fa\u003E - 4.3 ⭐\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003C\u002Fol\u003E\\n\u003Ch2\u003EPoki exclusive games\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EThese are exclusive browser games you won’t find anywhere else. Here are the 5 most popular original Poki games.\u003Cbr\u003E\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cul\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EDrive Mad: \u003C\u002Fstrong\u003EControl your car over uneven tracks where speed alone won’t save you. Balance and timing matter more than anything. This is the \u003Cstrong\u003Emost popular car game\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E on Poki, with a Poki Score of 10\u002F10.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ELevel Devil: \u003C\u002Fstrong\u003EStarts simple, then tricks you with fake platforms and sudden traps that catch you off guard.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ESubway Surfers: \u003C\u002Fstrong\u003EA smooth endless runner where dodging trains and obstacles keep your run alive.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003ERetro Bowl: \u003C\u002Fstrong\u003EA mix of action and management where every decision affects your progress across a full season.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Cstrong\u003EVortella’s Dress Up: \u003C\u002Fstrong\u003ECreate outfits, change styles and experiment with different looks in a chill multiplayer game.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003C\u002Ful\u003E\\n\u003Ch2\u003EReady to play?\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EWhat type of games are you in the mood for today? We let the world play with a variety of games where you can challenge yourself, relax, or play with friends.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EDiscover a massive library of games for boys and games for girls. There’s something for everyone!\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Ch3\u003EPlay with friends and others\u003C\u002Fh3\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EGrab a friend and play on the same keyboard or set up a private room to play online from anywhere, or compete against players from around the world!\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cul\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Ftwo-player\\\"\u003E2 player games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: In two player games, you can play side by side with a friend across \u003Cstrong\u003E130+ games\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E. Perfect for quick matches to settle who’s better straight away. No waiting, no matchmaking.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fio\\\"\u003Eio games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: Compete against other online players in io games. There are \u003Cstrong\u003E35+ games \u003C\u002Fstrong\u003Ewhere you start small, grow stronger, and try to outlast everyone else.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fmultiplayer\\\"\u003EMultiplayer games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: Play with more than 2 players or with other online players in our selection of multiplayer games. You’ll find \u003Cstrong\u003E150+ games\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E here to play.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003C\u002Ful\u003E\\n\u003Ch3\u003EChallenge yourself\u003C\u002Fh3\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EDo you have what it takes to take on the most challenging games on Poki? These games will test your driving skills, your shooting skills and much more.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cul\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fcar\\\"\u003ECar games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: With \u003Cstrong\u003E150+ games\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E to choose from, you can drift, race, or try to stay on track when things get messy. This is o\u003Cstrong\u003Ene of the most popular game categories\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E on Poki, with \u003Cstrong\u003E10% of all plays over 12 months\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E measured in our Game Intelligence research.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fshooting\\\"\u003EShooting games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: Aim fast and survive long enough to win the round before someone else takes you out. There are \u003Cstrong\u003E110+ games \u003C\u002Fstrong\u003Eto explore on Poki. Some games reward accurate aim, others are about how fast you can shoot.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fsports\\\"\u003ESports games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: Play games like basketball, soccer, football and more across \u003Cstrong\u003E150+ quick games \u003C\u002Fstrong\u003Ewhere every pass and every tackle matters.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fpuzzle\\\"\u003EPuzzle games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: Solve puzzles that make you think before you move across \u003Cstrong\u003E250+ games\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E. Puzzle games test how quickly you can notice what repeats and use it to win.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003C\u002Ful\u003E\\n\u003Ch3\u003EGames to relax\u003C\u002Fh3\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EEnjoy playing games where you can take your time and unwind. Let your creativity flourish in games where there is no timer or competition.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cul\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fdress-up\\\"\u003EDress up games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: Create outfits, switch styles, and keep changing clothes and makeup until you find a look that you like. Discover \u003Cstrong\u003E65+ games\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E on Poki!\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fdrawing\\\"\u003EDrawing games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: Sketch lines, color in pictures, solve some simple puzzles and unwind with relaxing drawing games. Explore \u003Cstrong\u003E30+ games\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E that let you play at your own pace.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003Cli\u003E\u003Ca href=\\\"https:\u002F\u002Fpoki.com\u002Fen\u002Fcooking\\\"\u003ECooking games\u003C\u002Fa\u003E: Prepare delicious meals from pizzas to milkshakes in \u003Cstrong\u003E30+\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E \u003Cstrong\u003Egames\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E. Take charge of a busy restaurant serving customers, or relax with cooking games where you cook meals without any pressure.\u003C\u002Fli\u003E\\n\u003C\u002Ful\u003E\\n\u003Ch2\u003EWhat is Poki?\u003C\u002Fh2\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EPoki is a platform where you can \u003Cstrong\u003Eplay free online games instantly\u003C\u002Fstrong\u003E in your browser. No installs, no downloads, just click and play on any device.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EWe’re a 65-person team based in Amsterdam, building Poki since 2014 to make playing games online as easy and fast as possible.\u003C\u002Fp\u003E\\n\u003Cp\u003EThat’s why we don’t just host browser games, we play them too. Every game is tested, tweaked, and genuinely enjoyed by the team to make sure it’s worth your class="SIe_wNXliOAsytkgDP1I">About Poki</div><header class="i3QPWr3keV_hOpU2xsic"><h1 class="Wa2Xpu5vfbxVCjRv2Gv_">Free online games on Poki</h1></header></div><div class="ExURFs1eybHeBRR0Ay6d"><div class="U5UHxulLIj1mqRNKVoIl S9u9oGGPKoCPdp0ckDId"><p>Play <strong>1500 free games</strong> instantly on Poki! All of the games are available to play on mobile, tablet and desktop.</p>
<p>Try driving games like Drift Boss, where one wrong turn sends you off the edge, or skill games like Stickman Hook, where perfect timing keeps your swing alive. There are also multiplayer games like Smash Karts, where you race and battle other players in real time.</p>
<p>Each month, over <strong>100 million players join Poki to play</strong>, share and find fun games to play on the web.</p>
<p>Wanna play? Start with what’s trending right now!</p>
<h2>Top free games</h2>
<p>These are the <strong>5 top trending games</strong> on Poki according to live stats on what’s being played the most right now.</p>
<ol>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/g/level-devil');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/g/level-devil">Level Devil</a> - 4.4⭐</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/g/smash-karts');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/g/smash-karts">Smash Karts</a> - 4.4⭐</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/g/subway-surfers');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/g/subway-surfers">Subway Surfers</a> - 4.4⭐</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/g/blocky-blast-puzzle');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/g/blocky-blast-puzzle">Blocky Blast Puzzle</a> - 4.3⭐</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/g/drive-mad');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/g/drive-mad">Drive Mad</a> - 4.3 ⭐</li>
</ol>
<h2>Poki exclusive games</h2>
<p>These are exclusive browser games you won’t find anywhere else. Here are the 5 most popular original Poki games.<br></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Drive Mad: </strong>Control your car over uneven tracks where speed alone won’t save you. Balance and timing matter more than anything. This is the <strong>most popular car game</strong> on Poki, with a Poki Score of 10/10.</li>
<li><strong>Level Devil: </strong>Starts simple, then tricks you with fake platforms and sudden traps that catch you off guard.</li>
<li><strong>Subway Surfers: </strong>A smooth endless runner where dodging trains and obstacles keep your run alive.</li>
<li><strong>Retro Bowl: </strong>A mix of action and management where every decision affects your progress across a full season.</li>
<li><strong>Vortella’s Dress Up: </strong>Create outfits, change styles and experiment with different looks in a chill multiplayer game.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Ready to play?</h2>
<p>What type of games are you in the mood for today? We let the world play with a variety of games where you can challenge yourself, relax, or play with friends.</p>
<p>Discover a massive library of games for boys and games for girls. There’s something for everyone!</p>
<h3>Play with friends and others</h3>
<p>Grab a friend and play on the same keyboard or set up a private room to play online from anywhere, or compete against players from around the world!</p>
<ul>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/two-player');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/two-player">2 player games</a>: In two player games, you can play side by side with a friend across <strong>130+ games</strong>. Perfect for quick matches to settle who’s better straight away. No waiting, no matchmaking.</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/io');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/io">io games</a>: Compete against other online players in io games. There are <strong>35+ games </strong>where you start small, grow stronger, and try to outlast everyone else.</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/multiplayer');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/multiplayer">Multiplayer games</a>: Play with more than 2 players or with other online players in our selection of multiplayer games. You’ll find <strong>150+ games</strong> here to play.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Challenge yourself</h3>
<p>Do you have what it takes to take on the most challenging games on Poki? These games will test your driving skills, your shooting skills and much more.</p>
<ul>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/car');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/car">Car games</a>: With <strong>150+ games</strong> to choose from, you can drift, race, or try to stay on track when things get messy. This is o<strong>ne of the most popular game categories</strong> on Poki, with <strong>10% of all plays over 12 months</strong> measured in our Game Intelligence research.</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/shooting');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/shooting">Shooting games</a>: Aim fast and survive long enough to win the round before someone else takes you out. There are <strong>110+ games </strong>to explore on Poki. Some games reward accurate aim, others are about how fast you can shoot.</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/sports');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/sports">Sports games</a>: Play games like basketball, soccer, football and more across <strong>150+ quick games </strong>where every pass and every tackle matters.</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/puzzle');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/puzzle">Puzzle games</a>: Solve puzzles that make you think before you move across <strong>250+ games</strong>. Puzzle games test how quickly you can notice what repeats and use it to win.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Games to relax</h3>
<p>Enjoy playing games where you can take your time and unwind. Let your creativity flourish in games where there is no timer or competition.</p>
<ul>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/dress-up');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/dress-up">Dress up games</a>: Create outfits, switch styles, and keep changing clothes and makeup until you find a look that you like. Discover <strong>65+ games</strong> on Poki!</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/drawing');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/drawing">Drawing games</a>: Sketch lines, color in pictures, solve some simple puzzles and unwind with relaxing drawing games. Explore <strong>30+ games</strong> that let you play at your own pace.</li>
<li><a onclick="window.navigateTo('/en/cooking');return false;" href="https://poki.com/en/cooking">Cooking games</a>: Prepare delicious meals from pizzas to milkshakes in <strong>30+</strong> <strong>games</strong>. Take charge of a busy restaurant serving customers, or relax with cooking games where you cook meals without any pressure.</li>
</ul>
<h2>What is Poki?</h2>
<p>Poki is a platform where you can <strong>play free online games instantly</strong> in your browser. No installs, no downloads, just click and play on any device.</p>
<p>We’re a 65-person team based in Amsterdam, building Poki since 2014 to make playing games online as easy and fast as possible.</p>
<p>That’s why we don’t just host browser games, we play them too. Every game is tested, tweaked, and genuinely enjoyed by the team to make sure it’s worth your time.</p></div></div><div class="v8TUHvsE2V93jQeU1nvV"></div></article></div><footer id="footer"
